# SPEC.md: DarkPyonix Ember

Functional (`FR-*`), non-functional (`NFR-*`) and protocol (`PR-*`) requirements. Every
implementation traces to an ID here; a requirement with no test is not done.

> **Revised 2026-10-03** to follow `INTENT.md`'s conversation-first, main-server model. The 09-22
> launcher section (§L) is rewritten. The IDE window (§W), its bridge (§B), the kernel (§K) and
> the editor-core draft (§E, formerly §M) are kept with small changes.
>
> Tags: **[user]**: from the user's brief. **[provisional]**: team proposal, not confirmed by
> the user; may be overturned. Requirements without a tag follow directly from a `[user]` decision
> in `INTENT.md`.

Areas: **L** launcher and conversation UI · **S** sessions and transcripts · **A** agent wrapping ·
**X** execution on computers · **T** agent-to-agent (A2A) · **R** remote and agent browser ·
**U** accounts and usage · **N** networking · **F** file transfer · **W** IDE window · **B** bridge · **K** kernel ·
**E** editor core (draft).

---

## Status (2026-10-04)

What exists on `develop`, checked against the code, CI and the issues. **CI** means the crate is
in `.github/workflows/test.yml`'s `cargo test` matrix (server, node, transport, hub, client,
bridge, editor-conn, editor), which passes on `develop`. `ember/app` (`ember-app`, the native UI)
is not in that matrix, because it pulls `dioxus-compose` as a git dependency; the session builds it.
Live agent tests (`EMBER_E2E_*`) and anything that needs a second computer, a real network, a
screen or a real hub are run by hand and are listed as not verified until they are.

| FR-S7 | v0 (system notice) implemented, CI (#34). Notice worded "now working on X (moved from Y)", never "invalid" (#131, `fr_l25_weekday_card_fits_chart_and_labels`; 1132 tests pass). Per-file hash comparison written (#79), not yet compiled or run. | #6 (v0); #79 |
| FR-S9–S11, FR-N7 (server API authentication) | Built (`INTENT.md` D37, [user] 2026-11-06, #338): P1 token file, server middleware, Host and Origin check, CDP nonce (`FR-S9`, `FR-S10`, #342); P2 client and app header (`FR-S11`, #347); P3 remote device path (`FR-N7`, no code change: the transport already gets the unwrapped router, tests `fr_n7_*`, #361); #368. CI runs the tests. The layer wraps the local TCP router only; the transport router is behind the device gate. Exempt from the token: `GET /api/health`, the agent routes that check a runtime token themselves (`/api/a2a/*` except `/api/a2a/settings`, the Antigravity hook, `POST /api/channels/<id>/post`) and `GET /auth/callback`. The agent browser relay is reached by MCP servers that send no header, so `GET /api/browsers/{project}/cdp/{nonce}` (and `.../json/version`) is exempt only for exactly that per-start nonce (constant time, in memory, never a query string) and still gets the Origin and Host check. `fr_s9_every_exempt_route_family_is_listed_and_nothing_else` walks every exempt family and its near misses (a path that only shares the `/api/a2a` prefix keeps the token). Open: rotation (#369), per-device scope (#366, `FR-N8`, provisional), same-user processes reading the token (own issue). | #338 |

Tests written, not yet run in CI. Questions are never answered automatically (see the FR-A5 requirement; tests `fr_a5_*`: merged (#667, #675), CI compiled and ran its tests; the follow-up tests named under FR-A5 are written, not yet compiled). | #73 |

| FR-L18 (Session screen), FR-L5–L7, FR-L15, FR-L16 (composer chips), FR-L24 (leader badge) | Implemented on `compose-rust` in `ember/app/src/ui/cr/{session,conversation}.rs` (pure model in `ember/app/src/ui/{session,conversation}.rs`, #162, #220), CI tests: side list with search and project sections (leader first), views 멀티 / 싱글 / 상태 saved per device, panes 2 per row on expanded and one at a time elsewhere, pane tabs saved per project, kanban and communication timeline from member status, IDE chooser with three entries and remembered default (`FR-L7`). Without drag and drop in the toolkit, panes are reordered with a move-left/right menu and added from the side list (GAPS: 앱 내부 드래그 앤 드롭); no split-on-edge. The terminal toggle shows a read-only, monospace view of the member's terminal in the mockup colours with a 10,000-line scrollback buffer and the last 500 lines drawn (pure model `ember/app/src/ui/term_pane.rs`, tests `fr_l18_terminal_*`): it attaches to the project's one running agent terminal through the server relay as a viewer (`read_only: true`, `active: false`: the node refuses its input and its window never resizes the shared terminal; the server does not enforce this, a viewer with less authority than the owner needs a server-enforced route), says so when the project has no running agent terminal or more than one (the node's terminal list carries no link to a member, so it does not guess), and shows the states connecting and failed (a fixed Korean sentence, never the transport's text); under `EMBER_DEMO_DATA` it shows sample output. Not built: text selection (compose-rust#5) and scrolling to the newest line. Placeholders ("준비 중입니다"): context, outputs, changes, web page list, model change on a running session. Status cards (#324): 부하 shows live session counts from the members; 오늘 토큰, PR과 CI and 서브 에이전트 스레드 show "아직 없습니다" until the server sends usage events (FR-U7) and a PR list with CI state, and show sample rows only under `EMBER_DEMO_DATA` (tests `fr_l18_status_*`). The cards draw typed rows (PR with CI pill, sub-agent tree, per-member token bars, computer bars); the computer bar and value stay empty without a server metric (#349). In a multi-column row the three status cards stretch to the tallest card's height (`Row::equal_height`); the one-sided border for the tree guide line is still open (compose-rust#464). The 웹 페이지 list (toolbar button) reads the node's listening ports (FR-P7, #386) for the session's computer; loopback ports open only on the local computer, a port with no known address says so, and a platform without a port list says so (tests `fr_l18_web_*`, `fr_l18_demo_ports_*`); written and tested (#326). At 560 dp and below the header keeps the 새 팀원, 버전 관리, 웹 페이지 and IDE buttons in its row as icons (no folded menu), the view segment moves to its own row with icons only, and a one-pane layout is a sideways-scrolling strip of panes, each the column width less the 8 dp gap and a 24 dp peek so the next one shows at the right edge (the mockup's `calc(100% - 8px)` equals its gap, so its peek is 0); the 24 dp peek is a stand-in until compose-rust#547 adds scroll snapping, after which the strip centres each pane as the mockup does (`calc(100% - 8px)`, 4 dp each side), in place of a previous / next pager; from 561 to 699 dp panes stack one per row, as the mockup's `.mv` column (mockup `session.css` at 560 px; tests `fr_l18_phone_header_*`, `fr_l18_one_pane_layout_*`, `fr_l18_swipe_pane_width_*`, `fr_l18_multi_layout_*`, #512); the toolkit has no scroll snapping (compose-rust#547), so a swipe can stop between panes. Below 901 dp the session list opens as a left drawer over the session content (beside the 64 px rail at tablet width, in the list column's place) with a scrim that closes it, width `min(300, 86% of the window)` at phone width and 290 on tablet (tests `fr_l18_drawer_*`, #582); the phone tab bar and title strip stay outside the overlay, the drawer state resets when the list is a column again, and Escape does not close it (compose-rust#476); the toggle, scrim and drawer have no accessible name yet (compose-rust#429). The 버전 관리 button (toolbar button) opens the version control screen as a large sheet over the session for the session's project, with the same git data, without changing the selected project or leaving the conversation; the sheet follows the mockup popup (`session.js` vcsOpen, `session.css` `.svcs-box`): 90 percent of the window width (capped at 1360) and about 90 percent of its height at 560 dp and wider, so the session stays visible behind the scrim, and full screen below 560 dp; a window size that is not reported yet (0 dp in the first frame) is treated as unknown (last known size, else 1024 x 720) and corrected on the next composition; the sheet scrolls as a page (stacked panes, tabs on compact) and `EMBER_CR_SESSION_VCS=1` opens it at start (tests `fr_l18_vcs_sheet_*`, #495). The shared sheet sets zero dialog padding so the frame includes its header within the 90 percent height (#642); the VCS sheet renderer dismiss callback has a host test. The phone full-screen presentation still depends on a reported window size (compose-rust#467, #479), and the renderer dialog may impose platform margins; full-screen rendering and scrolling at 390 dp are not yet verified on a display. The close button, scrim and Escape are not separately verified on a display (Escape is open in compose-rust#476). Written, not compiled by the author. | #162, #220, #324 (visual match with the mockup: #349) |
| FR-L12 (Channels screen) | Implemented on `compose-rust` in `ember/app/src/ui/cr/channels.rs` (pure model in `ember/app/src/ui/channels.rs`, #167, #220), CI tests: grouping by project, filter and search, KakaoTalk-style room, compact list/detail switch. The screen reads the server (CI builds and tests it; tests `fr_l12_*`, `fr_t10_*` in `ember/app/src/ui/channels.rs` and `ui/cr/channels.rs`): the room list from `GET /api/channels`, the open room's messages, polling `?after=seq` every 2 s while a room is open (it stops when the room or the screen closes), sending with `POST`, the person (`user`) shown as 나, and honest loading, failed and empty states. Demo mode keeps the sample rooms. The unread badge shows the server's unread count; a room opened by a click is marked read, an auto-selected room is not (#509). The `+` button opens the 새 채널 sheet (#499, `ui/cr/new_channel.rs`, pure model `NewChannelDraft` in `ui/channels.rs`, tests `fr_t10_new_channel_*`, **not yet compiled or run**): name (required, trimmed, no control, format or line-separator character, a name already used in the project is refused), project chips (the list filter or the first project; the default project is allowed), the project's agent sessions as chips (none chosen means no agent, and the sheet says so), 만들기 off until valid, an unsaved-changes question on close, and on success the list is read again and the new room opens. A failure shows a fixed Korean sentence chosen by status and `code`, never the server's text. In demo mode the button tells "데모에서는 채널을 만들 수 없습니다" and creates nothing. The server does not enforce a unique name or a length limit, so the taken-name check is the client's and the sentence for a 409 is ready for a server that does. The button has the accessible name 새 채널 (overlay button, compose-rust#425, #429). Not built: push instead of polling, unread counts, `FR-T11`, `FR-T12` (@ mention, member picker, "reaches agent" marker), `FR-T13`. A send keeps the draft until the server answers Ok and blocks a second send while one is in flight. The icon-only buttons (back, members, menu, +, emoji) have no accessible names yet: `compose-rust` has no accessibility modifier, so they wait for compose-rust#429 (`TODO(compose-rust#429)` in `ui/cr/channels.rs`). The owner sheet for invites, guests and allowances (FR-T14, FR-T15, FR-T17) opens from the members button; see the FR-T12–T15 row. The list title is 18 Semibold at every width and a room row is 64 high with a 44 icon (tests `fr_l12_channels_title_is_18_at_every_width`, `fr_l12_channels_row_is_64_high`). | #167, #220, #331 |
| FR-T10, FR-T16 (partly) | Server API built in `ember/server/src/channels/` (#331), written test first, compiled and run by CI, passing on develop (2026-11-06) (tests `fr_t10_*`, `fr_t16_*` in `ember/server/tests/fr_t10_channels.rs`): `GET`/`POST /api/channels`, `GET /api/channels/{id}`, `GET`/`POST /api/channels/{id}/messages?after=`. Default channel per project (members: all its sessions), created channels keep their chosen sessions, durable log in the main store (SQLite). A posted message is pushed as `channel_message` on `/api/push` (written test first, test `fr_t10_a_posted_message_is_pushed_to_websocket_clients`, compiled and run by CI, passing on develop (2026-11-06)); the client skips that type for now. Read tracking: table `channel_reads` (one cursor for the reader `user`; per-person readers wait on `FR-T14`), `POST /api/channels/{id}/read {seq?}`, and `unread` on a channel (messages after the cursor not written by `user`) and on a message (1 or 0), written test first (`fr_t10_unread_*`, `fr_t10_mark_read_*`, `fr_t10_read_of_an_unknown_channel_is_404`), compiled and run by CI, passing on develop (2026-11-06); the unread count is not yet pushed on its own, and a client re-reads the list. Human messages (`from == user`) are also appended to `<data dir>/channels/<channel id>.jsonl` (`FR-T16`, written test first, `ember/server/tests/fr_t16_jsonl.rs`, compiled and run by CI, passing on develop (2026-11-06)): one line `{seq, channel_id, from, text, at}`, one append per line, never rewritten, agent messages are not written, the channel id is checked as a safe path component; SQLite stays the read index. A post inserts the row, then appends the line (under one lock, so lines follow `seq`), and deletes the row and returns 500 if the append fails. Line schema and path stay [provisional]. Start-up recovery and hardening of the file are written test first, NOT yet compiled or run (see `FR-T16`). Not built: repair of a row missing from the middle of the file, per-person read cursors, the client reading the API. | #331 |
| FR-T11 (partly) | Server feed built in `ember/server/src/channels/feed.rs` (#331), written test first, compiled and run by CI, passing on develop (2026-11-06) (tests `fr_t11_*` in `ember/server/tests/fr_t11_feed.rs`): `GET /api/channels/{id}/feed` lists, newest first, the member sessions' pending approvals (`kind: approval`, `approval_id`, `tool`, `input`) and last-turn reports (`kind: report`, `outcome` completed, failed or interrupted, `summary`, `error`; shown while the session is finished or failed), each with `agent` (id, name, harness), `session_id` and `link`. It is derived from the session events and status, with no store of its own. `POST /api/channels/{id}/feed/{approval_id}/answer {decision}` calls `Sessions::answer` for the owning member session (404 when the approval is not pending in this channel); the session records the resolution, so both clear together. Pending questions are listed (`kind: question`, merged (#667, #675), CI compiled and ran its tests; see FR-T11) and answered through `POST /api/channels/{id}/questions/{question_id}/answer` (written, not yet compiled; see FR-T11). Not built: stall reports, dismissing a report, a feed push (a client refetches on the `event` pushes of member sessions), the client reading the feed. The approval of a question tool also carries a readable `questions` field (tests `fr_t11_questions_*`, `fr_t11_a_question_approval_*`, `fr_t11_a_malformed_question_*`, merged (#667, #675), CI compiled and ran its tests). |  #331 |

| FR-U7 (tokens per session and day) | Implemented on the server (#332): `ember/server/src/usage.rs` totals the `usage` events already stored (input and output tokens per reported turn), `GET /api/usage/summary?days=N` or `?day=YYYY-MM-DD` (optional `project`) per UTC day and per harness, `GET /api/sessions/{id}/usage` and `usage` in `GET /sessions/{id}`. Tests `fr_u7_*` in `ember/server/tests/fr_u7_usage.rs` and `src/usage.rs`. Cost is `null` with `cost_note`: no price table is specified and none is invented. Client (#332): `Api::usage_summary` and `Api::session_usage` with tolerant decode (`ember/client/src/api.rs`), the pure model `ember/app/src/ui/usage.rs` (1.2M / 3.4K, "알 수 없음" for a session whose harness reported nothing, "UTC 기준" beside every day, empty and failed states), fetched at screen open and every 30 s by `bridge.rs`; Home, 에이전트 and Session show it (the Home 하네스별 사용량 tile draws the 7-day token stack per harness and keeps the session counts below it, #360; tests `fr_u7_stack_*`, `fr_u7_weekday_names_*`). Server agent names map to labels: claude-code to Claude Code, codex to Codex, antigravity to agy, any other name as is. The cost is never shown: it stays unknown while `cost_usd` is `null`. Not verified: nothing was compiled or run. | #332 |
| FR-L11 (Home), FR-L15 (model picker), FR-L16 (mode picker), FR-L23 (dashboard) | Implemented on `compose-rust` (#158, #220): the Home screen in `ember/app/src/ui/cr/home.rs` (pure model in `ember/app/src/ui/home.rs`), wired as 홈, CI tests. Chips, pickers, send-creates-a-member and the dashboard tiles the client has data for (running members, online computers, success rate from ended sessions, sessions per harness, working crew, projects). The old 하네스별 세션 tile (session rings) was intentionally replaced by the 하네스별 사용량 token stack (#360), because the mockup's single tile shows tokens per harness; session counts stay as a small line under the chart. Token trend tile is live (#332): tokens per UTC day for every harness, 7 or 14 days, from `GET /api/usage/summary`, with "비용은 알 수 없습니다". The card (#509, tests `fr_l25_weekday_card_fits_chart_and_labels`, `fr_l25_weekday_bars_geometry`) opens on 14 days, has a 42x26 toggle pill, a dot legend in the head (a 비용 dot only when cost data exists and a hatched 토큰 dot drawn with the bars' own hatch (grey.strong, same step, `legend_hatch_style`; the mockup has no 오늘 entry, so the orange today bar has none), hidden when `layout().compact` is true, i.e. a window of 560 dp or less, so a tokens-only card on a phone has no legend by design; the dots have no accessible label, TODO(compose-rust#428, #429); tests `fr_u7_cost_legend_entries_follow_data`, `fr_l23_cost_card_legend_hidden_when_narrow`, `fr_u7_legend_dot_matches_the_bar_hatch`), and four stats (기간 비용, 토큰, 작업당 비용, 캐시 적중) from the summary: cost is the sum of daily `cost_usd`, cost per job is that divided by the daily session counts, and any stat without data reads "데이터 없음" (cache hits always, until the `usage` event carries cache tokens); no hover tooltip or bar tap (toolkit); tests `fr_l11_cost_card_*` in `ember/app/src/ui/usage.rs`. The mode picker (FR-L16, #507) lists the harness's modes in the mockup order (ask, edits, bypass, plan) under a "권한 모드 · harness" header, each with a description line, a check on the chosen row and the bypass row in red with a warning triangle. The second tap of an irreversible choice counts after 600 ms (`AMEND_CONFIRM_DELAY_MS`), so a fast double tap cannot skip the question. Bypass needs a second tap (the row first reads "다시 눌러 바이패스 적용" and closing the picker withdraws it), and while it is on the composer's mode chip is drawn in the warning state. Effort names are Korean (낮음, 보통, 높음; tests `fr_l15_effort_*`, `fr_l16_*`). At 561 px and wider both pickers are popovers under their chip (`Menu` places its popup below the anchor since compose-rust v0.0.5, #383); the model popover's width is clamped to what the window leaves beside the navigation (`popover_width`, tests `fr_l15_popover_width_*`); at 560 px and below both stay modals. Pointing at another row withdrawing the bypass confirmation is modelled (`mode_hover`) but not wired, because the toolkit has no hover event on a row. Not built: cost (no price table) and approval wait (tiles say 준비 중입니다), favourites (rail group and stars), `Ctrl+number` key handling, profile names (FR-L17), the server model list (a local static list is used), a permission mode and effort on `POST /sessions` (only bypass is applied, through approve-all), integrations. Honest states (#498, #500): the dashboard has a 연동 area with a GitHub card (connected to `GET /api/integrations/github` by `FR-L35`: pill 준비 중입니다 until the first answer; the pure model maps that body, shows 연결됨, 재인증 필요 or 연결 안 됨, the server's reason, at most 3 repositories with a 14-cell CI bar, and a 방법 보기 control that only shows `gh auth login` guidance; a null value reads "알 수 없음" when connected and "아직 정보가 없습니다" when not, never 0) and five small cards (Slack, Discord, Linear, 캘린더, 메일) that say "12월 이후에 지원합니다"; no count or number the server did not send; tests `fr_l35_github_card_*`, `fr_l35_github_state_decodes_the_server_body`, `fr_l23_other_integrations_are_coming_soon`; the area follows the mockup `.int-grid` (above 1279 the GitHub card beside the small cards in two columns, at 1279 and below the GitHub card full width, at 560 and below one column; test `fr_l23_integrations_grid_follows_the_mockup_breakpoints`), and the GitHub card carries the `CI 실패 -> 루틴` rule row with a 루틴 보기 link and the note that it does not run yet, because the server has no field tying a CI failure to a routine and no event trigger (test `fr_l23_ci_rule_links_to_routines_and_says_it_does_not_run`) [provisional, #498]; the composer attach button opens an inline absolute local file path entry; readable files are staged as removable filename chips, and sending adds their paths to the prompt and clears the chips after session creation. File contents are not uploaded, and paths may not be accessible on the selected computer. Empty and invalid paths show an error. A native file picker still needs compose-rust#483 [provisional, #500]. The send button is the shared ink circle with the paper plane (`SendCircleButton`, always ink, no grey disabled state; an empty input does nothing), and the composer and suggestion chips are 32 px pills with a glyph, a 13 px weight 500 label and the picker chevron instead of toolkit buttons (tests `fr_l10_home_send_button_*`, not yet compiled). | #158, #220 |
| FR-L17, FR-L20 (에이전트) | Implemented on `compose-rust` (#173, #220, #244): the screen in `ember/app/src/ui/cr/agents.rs` (pure model in `ember/app/src/ui/agents.rs`), wired as 에이전트, CI tests. Section nav (하네스, 모델, 프로필, 네트워크, 사용량), harness cards from the detected agents (installed, version), run mode, the Antigravity sandbox (off by default, FR-A5e), the resident shim (not for Antigravity), memory limit, default profile; default model, effort and mode per harness from the Home catalog; profile cards with account order (up/down buttons instead of drag), switch threshold, routing policy, failure handling and a preview of the account a new session starts on. Settings and profiles have no server API, except the per-harness memory limit (`GET /api/agents/settings`, `PUT /api/agents/{agent}/settings`, `FR-L20`) and the new-member defaults (`GET /api/agents/defaults`, `PUT /api/agents/defaults`, `FR-L20`, #481, #670), so the remaining per-harness switches and profiles are kept on this device only and the screen says so (TODO: server store). Network shows the computers' reachability; the egress switch, per-computer IPs, cost, model usage and account rings say 준비 중입니다 (no endpoint). **Honest harness cards (P0-3, not yet compiled or run; tests `fr_l20_*`, `fr_l21_*` in `ember/app/src/ui/agents.rs`):** the KPI row is derived, never invented: installed from the detected agents, memory total as the sum of the limits set on installed harnesses (this device's settings), last detection from the time the app last got an answer from `GET /agents`; the resident shim count has no client source (TODO(server)). A value without a source reads 아직 정보가 없습니다. Sample captures (`EMBER_CR_ROUTE=agents`) flag their numbers 예시. A harness shows a version only when the server reported one (an installed harness with none reads 버전을 알 수 없습니다), and a path only when the server reported one (`GET /agents` `path`, or the detect answer); otherwise 아직 정보가 없습니다. No path is hard-coded; sample captures show sample paths marked 예시, and demo versions and counts carry 예시. 다시 찾기 calls `POST /api/computers/local/harnesses/detect` (`Api::detect_harnesses`, `FR-A6.1`; `bridge::rescan_agents`), applies installed, version, path and the probe time (the real 마지막 감지) to the cards, and keeps the button label (찾는 중입니다 while it runs). The outcome shows once beside the buttons and clears after a few seconds: 찾았습니다: N개, 새로 찾은 하네스가 없습니다, 찾지 못했습니다, 이 컴퓨터에서는 아직 다시 찾을 수 없습니다 (501 `not_implemented`) or a fixed failure sentence. Demo mode re-reads its canned list and marks the sentence 예시. The Gemini card reads 준비 중입니다 with 다시 찾기 disabled and shows no value until the server has a Gemini CLI adapter (`INTENT.md` D40, `FR-A10`). **Computers, resets and defaults (not yet compiled or run; tests `fr_l20_*`, `fr_x6_*` in `ember/app/src/ui/agents.rs`):** a computer tile shows the name and reachability from the client; its OS, IP, session count and open-connection count have no client source, so they read 아직 정보가 없습니다 (never 0). The name-keyed sample table (`computer_facts`) is used only in sample captures and every value is marked 예시. The "connection lost, N sessions move to X" sentence appears only for a computer that is really offline with a known session count and fallback (sample only today). An account tile shows a reset time only when a source exists (the accounts API has none, so 리셋 시각 아직 정보가 없습니다; sample marked 예시). **Defaults and 계정 추가 (P0 #481, #670; tests `fr_l20_*`, `fr_l17_*`, `fr_u1_*`):** the two default chips on 모델 show only what is stored. The harness chip reads 아직 정하지 않았습니다 until the owner chose a harness and model in its picker (or loaded it from `GET /api/agents/defaults`), never the seeded Home catalog value; the picker lists detected harnesses only and disables the others with the reason (설치되지 않았습니다, 준비 중입니다). Choosing a default harness, model, effort or mode saves it to the server through `PUT /api/agents/defaults` (`ServerApi::put_agent_defaults`, `FR-L20`, #670), and the card says 고른 하네스와 모델, 기본 계정은 서버에 저장되어 새 팀원에 적용됩니다 (`DEFAULTS_STORAGE_NOTE`). The account chip shows the default account of the chosen harness (`is_default`), reads 계정이 없습니다 with no account, and opens a sheet of the real accounts; choosing calls `POST /api/accounts/{id}/default` (`ServerApi::set_default_account`, FR-L17) and re-reads `GET /api/accounts`. 계정 추가 is honest: pick a harness (Antigravity and ACP are disabled with the server's own note) and a label, `POST /api/accounts` creates the account, the returned login command shows as selectable text with the server's note, and 로그인 확인 calls `POST /api/accounts/{id}/check` and shows 로그인되어 있습니다, 아직 로그인하지 않았습니다 or 로그인 상태를 알 수 없습니다 (FR-U1). A failed call shows a fixed Korean sentence chosen by HTTP status (`account_error_ko`, test `fr_l17_account_errors_are_fixed_korean`), never the client error text. The app opens no login window; the login runs on the server's machine (INTENT E3) and the sheet says so. The command is never logged. Demo mode shows samples marked 예시 and calls nothing. The plan marks in the model catalog are Ember's own reference list, not server data, and the column says so (플랜별 사용 (Ember 참고 목록)). The 고정 버전 text uses `PINNED_ANTIGRAVITY_VERSION`. Tests `fr_l20_*`, `fr_a6_1_*`. Tokens today count only the ChatGPT plan accounts; the 7-day total and Home's 7/14-day token bars come from `GET /api/usage` (`FR-U7`, #184). | #173, #184, #220, #481, #670 |

| FR-L25 (루틴), NFR-L4 | Implemented on `compose-rust` (#169, #220, #244): the screen in `ember/app/src/ui/cr/routines.rs` (pure model in `ember/app/src/ui/routines.rs`), wired as 루틴, CI tests. Dashboard (four KPIs, success ring, weekday bars), list grouped by project with filter pills and search, detail, and the form (trigger, cron or event, model and mode chips from Home, computer, prompt; the profile follows the harness, no account select). Routines are the server's schedules (FR-A8): `ember-client` wraps `/api/schedules` (list, create, edit, pause, resume, run now, run history; test `ember/client/tests/schedules_flow.rs`), and the screen lists, creates, edits, pauses, resumes and runs them and shows run history through it (#183). The server stores the routine's name in `ScheduleTarget::New { title }` (falling back to the first line of the prompt when `title` is empty); model and mode show the harness defaults, and event routines stay in the in-memory `LocalStore`, which is also the fallback while the server cannot be reached (the screen says so). `PATCH /api/schedules/{id}` (`SchedulePatch`) supports updating `project`, `agent`, `computer` (an empty string clears `computer_id` to `None`), `cwd`, `target` (`ScheduleTarget::New { title }`), `prompt`, `kind` and `catch_up`, and the edit form pre-fills the stored `name`, `cron` (or event) and `prompt` directly into the `EmberField` `text` option (`FieldOptions.text`) as well as the stored `project`, `harness`, `model`, `mode` and `computer` in the pickers (#493; tests `fr_l25_edit_patch_*`, `fr_l25_edit_form_is_prefilled_from_the_routine`, `fr_l25_host_edit_form_prefills_fields_and_allows_editing`). Because `Schedule` stores only the harness (`agent`) and neither a per-schedule model nor a permission mode, and a `ScheduleTarget::Continue` routine has no title on the server, `unsaved_fields` reports any change to model, permission mode, or the name of a `Continue`-target routine with `not_saved_sentence`, and `changed_patch` preserves `Continue` targets (`tests/mcp_schedules.rs` `fr_a8_schedule_patch_updates_fields_and_validates_constraints`). The detail panel gives its prompt and tiles the full panel width (inset panels fill their width), puts the three tiles in one row from a 520 dp panel and stacks them below it, and below 420 dp moves the toggle, 편집 and 지금 실행 under the title so none is clipped (`detail_header_layout`, #492). On a server routine, 지금 실행 (`remote_run_now`, #494) immediately inserts an optimistic `RunStatus::Running` row ("실행 중"), calls `POST /api/schedules/{id}/run`, refreshes `GET /sessions` into the client state when a session is created, replaces the placeholder with the server's `ScheduleRun` (`apply_server_run_started`), and polls the schedule runs and sessions every 1 s (`REMOTE_RUN_POLL_MS`) while any remote run is `Running` (`has_running_remote_run`); if the call fails, the optimistic placeholder is rolled back (`rollback_optimistic_run`), the server list is re-synced, and the server's error is shown without fake success; while the routine has a run in progress (the optimistic row or a server run still `Running`), 지금 실행 is disabled and reads 실행 중 beside a spinner, so a second click cannot start a second run (`run_now_busy`); `세션 열기` opens the run's conversation (`resolve_run_session_id` maps sample `s1`..`s9` to `demo-s1`..`demo-s9` in demo mode and keeps server session ids as-is; tests `fr_l25_apply_server_run_started_*`, `fr_l25_has_running_remote_run_*`, `fr_l25_remote_run_now_live_progress_and_error_rollback`, `fr_l25_host_open_session_button_resolves_demo_session_and_opens_conversation`). In demo mode (`EMBER_DEMO_DATA`) 지금 실행 simulates a run: a "실행 중" entry, then after 2.5 s a finished entry and a new sample session titled "(예시)", every simulated entry flagged 예시 (`begin_demo_run`, `finish_demo_run`, #494); without the demo and without a server the honest toast stays. Where the list and the detail sit side by side, new and edit open as an inline form in the detail area (title, 취소 and 저장 in its header; `form_layout`, mockup v8) and the compact layouts keep the sheet; the fields are name, project, trigger, 일정 설명 (read only, derived from the schedule, the server stores no description), cron or event, one model picker (harness, model, effort), a 권한 모드 select, computer and prompt (#511). An event routine's big heading wraps to three lines. Cron is read in UTC (no time-zone source yet). A finished run reads 완료 (not 성공, which stays the name of the rate), and the weekday bars are 150 dp tall (120 narrow) pill bars in a rolling week ending today with today in bold (tests `fr_l25_routine_run_pill_says_done`, `fr_l25_weekday_bars_geometry`, `fr_l25_weekday_card_fits_chart_and_labels`; 1132 tests pass). | #169, #220, #493, #494 |

The 팀 작업 tab (one row per worktree with the member whose session folder is in it, branch, folder, ahead/behind and the pull request the 풀 리퀘스트 section has read) is written, not compiled by the author (#482). The 풀 리퀘스트 tab shows the selected pull request in full (checks, reviewers, comments, a review box, 병합 asking twice and carrying the head SHA it showed, and a 새 PR form) through FR-G10 to FR-G12 (#483); written and tested, real gh not verified. The 히스토리 tab selects a commit and shows its detail (message, author, time, parents, SHA, refs, the changed files with status letter and +/- counts) with 여기서 브랜치, 되돌리기 and Cherry-pick (the last two ask twice, name the branch that gets the commit and show the node's Korean answers), SHA 복사 with system clipboard copy and selectable fallback (compose-rust#471), header filter chips (모든 브랜치, 로컬 전용), and 푸시 안 됨 and 원격에 있음 status pills (#645); the lane graph draws an indicator dot while full edge curves wait for compose-rust#308. Error mapping converts raw git errors to Korean sentences. Written and tested (app #645). While a merge is in progress the 충돌 section shows a banner ('<from> -> <into> 병합 중', the remaining conflict files, 병합 취소 and 병합 커밋 each asking twice; 병합 커밋 is enabled only when no conflict file is left and notes that staged changes go into the commit) and the file editor has 모두 현재 것 and 모두 들어오는 것 (asking twice when a block already has a choice); written and tested (#382); not verified by clicks, with a real merge, or the banner's placement against the mockup (the buttons sit below the text, no owner line). Control text sizes follow the small scale (13 px, `VCS_CONTROL_FS`, like the mockup's `.btn.sm`, `.chip` and `.field.sm`) except where noted (audit P2, #517; test `fr_l19_control_text_uses_the_small_scale`, written, not compiled by the author): the commit summary and description fields and the new-branch field take the `Label` text role, the PR filter chips and the 이 덩어리 전체 button are painted pills with a 13 px label (the hunk button smaller, with a 1 px border), and the stash pills already were. Still toolkit size (16 px) because compose-rust v0.0.6 `Button` has no font size (TODO(compose-rust#500)): the filled `AppButton`s (새 브랜치, 커밋, 병합, 목록으로, the conflict-editor buttons), the section and tab chips, and the new-PR form fields. The 충돌 list rows are left-aligned with a warning mark and a gap under the explanation (audit P2; written and tested). | #168, #517 |

| NFR-N1 | Loopback measurements and the 20-run matrix harness are available; the WAN network matrix is not yet measured. See `docs/design/NFR-N1-WAN-MEASUREMENT.md`. The letify-link backend has a loopback measurement (`tests/letify_perf.rs`, Mac mini, release, 256 MiB, 2026-11-01): one stream 340 MiB/s (raw TCP 5407 MiB/s, 6.3%), four streams 339 MiB/s in total; a 32 byte round trip on an idle connection p50 0.047 ms, p95 0.062 ms; during a bulk transfer on a bulk-class stream p50 0.437 ms, p95 0.547 ms (interactive-class transfer: p50 0.589 ms, p95 0.701 ms); first byte through the main server relay on loopback 21 ms. That was letify-link 4efccb2; with 4363cfb (ring-accelerated crypto, one seal and open task per subflow) one stream carries 961 MiB/s (18.2% of raw TCP), four streams 955 MiB/s in total, and a small message during a bulk transfer has p99 0.065 ms. All of it is far above any WAN rate. Same test on the iroh backend: one stream 258 MiB/s (raw TCP 5815 MiB/s, 4.4%), idle p50 0.042 ms, during a bulk-class transfer p50 0.762 ms, p95 4.797 ms, p99 7.447 ms; so letify-link carries about 1.3 times the throughput of iroh and a tenth of its small-message p99 under bulk. | #10, #229 |

| FR-L7 and FR-W4 (IDE computers, #454) | `GET /api/sessions/{id}/ide` takes its computers from the computers registry (the local computer and every node) and the session's current computer. `launch.computer` and `computer.name` are the registry id (what `/api/computers/{id}/terms` expects); `computer_name` is added beside them (absent without a registry). `?computer=` accepts a registry id, a registry name or an `EMBER_IDE_COMPUTERS` name; the default is the session's current computer, else `default_computer`, else the local computer. `EMBER_IDE_COMPUTERS` is an override: an entry named like a registry id or name replaces that computer's IDE details (the id stays the registry id), any other entry keeps working with id = name and one warning. A non-local computer's Ember target stays `available:false` ("OSE server is only available on the local computer"). Tests `ember/server/tests/fr_w4_ide_computers.rs` (written, not yet run by CI at the time of writing). | #454 |
| FR-W4 (local computer terminals, #455) | `/api/computers/local/terms` works like any computer's: the server lazily starts ONE in-process node for its own machine (`computers/local_node.rs`, `Computers::local_node_client`) on an axum listener bound to `127.0.0.1:0`, roots `$HOME`, built on the first terminal use (a server that never opens one binds nothing); the listener task is aborted when `Computers` drops, which closes the port. Security: this adds a loopback HTTP listener on the server machine. Its Bearer token is 32 bytes from the OS random source (`getrandom`, 256 bits, 64 hex characters; it was two UUIDv4 strings, about 244 random bits, until #474), held in memory, never logged or written. The client ignores proxy variables (`NodeClient::new_local`, #474). Another local OS user can connect to the port but cannot authenticate without the token. The node serves file and terminal operations as the server user within its roots, so the token equals the server token in power. It is not exposed through the app router, the guest door or the transport; a request-supplied value reaches it only through the existing relay routes, which stay behind `auth::protect`. `api/git.rs` keeps its own in-process node (not merged yet). Tests `fr_w4_local_terms_*` in `ember/server/tests/fr_w4_local_terms.rs`, CI. | #455 |
| FR-W4 and FR-X1 (denied paths, #521) | The local node's roots stay `$HOME`, but it now DENIES the server data dir (`EMBER_DATA_DIR`, else `~/.ember`) and the OSE data root (`EMBER_OSE_DATA_DIR`, else `<data dir>/ose-data`) when that is a different path: `PathPolicy::with_denied(roots, denied)` in `ember/node/src/policy.rs`, `NodeConfig::with_denied`, `denied_paths` in `computers/local_node.rs`. A canonical path that equals or is inside a denied entry (a symlink into it, `..` into it, a write target below it, a search that would walk into it) is refused with the same 403 `forbidden_path` as a path outside the roots, so the answer does not reveal the exclusion. A path that only shares a prefix (`~/.ember-other`) is allowed. Consequence: a terminal or git repository cannot START with its cwd inside the data dir (the cwd check goes through the policy). A real remote node denies nothing unless `EMBER_NODE_DENY` (path list, like `PATH`) is set. **Honesty:** `PathPolicy` is not a sandbox. Terminals and agents run as the server user and can read `~/.ember` by shell (and `cd` there), so this only narrows the `/fs` API read exposure of `server-token`, `ember.db` and the OSE token files; D37 full authority is unchanged. Tests `fr_x1_policy_denied_*` (`ember/node/tests/policy_denied.rs`, unit tests in `policy.rs` and `config.rs`) and `fr_w4_local_node_denied_*` (`ember/server/tests/fr_w4_local_node_denied.rs`), CI. | #521 |
| FR-W4 (OSE token directory, #521) | The tokens dir `<ose data root>/<computer id>/tokens` and its parent computer dir are hardened at every OSE start (`secure_token_dir` in `ember/server/src/ose.rs`): an existing directory owned by the current user that group or others can write (`mode & 0o022`) REFUSES the start with `token directory <path> is writable by others; fix the permissions` and no token file is written; one with only group/other read or search bits is tightened to 0700 (logged at warn); a directory owned by another user or a symlink is refused. The shared data root above keeps its old behaviour (warn only). The token file on Windows keeps the default ACL (not hardened): any process of the same user, and an administrator, can read it, the same exposure as `server-token`. *[provisional, `INTENT.md` D45]* At startup the server removes every regular `<hex>.token` file under `<ose data root>/<computer id>/tokens/` (`OseManager::sweep_stale_tokens`, called from `main.rs` before any OSE start), because a SIGKILL skips the token file's drop; symlinks, directories and other names stay. A second server sharing the same OSE data root loses its live OSE's file too; that OSE read the file at its start, so it keeps working. The local node's exclusion of `~/.ember` and the OSE data root (#521 item 4) is the `FR-W4 and FR-X1 (denied paths)` row above (#525). UNVERIFIED with a real `dpx-ose-server` (owner): that it reads `--connection-token-file` once at start and strips a trailing newline. Tests `fr_w4_ose_token_dir_*` in `ember/server/tests/fr_w4_ose_token_dir.rs` (the sweep: `fr_w4_ose_token_dir_startup_sweep_removes_stale_token_files`), not yet compiled or run. Since #534 the check and the chmod go through one handle opened with `O_NOFOLLOW | O_DIRECTORY` (`fstat`, `fchmod`), so a symlink swapped in after the check cannot redirect the chmod; ACLs are not inspected (written, not yet compiled or run). | #521, #534 |
| FR-X1 (open, then check, #468) | *[provisional, 2026-11-13]* The node file API no longer opens a path it checked earlier. Content operations open first and check the opened handle (`PathPolicy::check_opened` in `ember/node/src/policy.rs`, `open_checked` in `ember/node/src/fs.rs`): the path is resolved again after the open and, on Unix, the handle's device and inode must equal the resolved path's, else the answer is the 403 `forbidden_path` of a path outside the roots. On Unix the final component opens with `O_NOFOLLOW`. Covered: `read` (the hash comes from the same handle), `pwrite`, `setattr` (size, and on Unix mode and times through `fchmod` and `futimens`; a file this user cannot open for reading falls back to the path), `grep` (`search_file` on the checked handle), and on Unix the atomic `write`, which opens the target directory with `O_NOFOLLOW | O_DIRECTORY`, checks it, and creates, hashes and renames inside it (`openat`, `fstatat`, `renameat`). **Residual (recorded, not fixed):** namespace operations (`mkdir`, `remove`, `rename`, `symlink`, `stat`, `lstat`, `readlink`, `list`, `glob`) still act on paths, so a parent directory swapped between their check and their system call can redirect them; on Windows `write` stays path based and `check_opened` only resolves again (no stable file id in std). Under D37 an admitted device already has full authority over its own files, so the residual matters only for the narrowing of the local node and denied paths. Decision: the server write relay stays limited to what is covered here (`write`, `pwrite`, `setattr`) until the namespace operations move to directory handles. Tests `fr_x1_*` in `ember/node/tests/fs_toctou.rs` race a symlink swap against read, grep, write, and a final-component swap against read and pwrite; unit test `fr_x1_check_opened_*` in `policy.rs`. Written, not yet compiled or run. | #468 |
| FR-X1 (denied paths follow-ups, #534) | *[provisional, 2026-11-13]* (1) `/fs/list` leaves out a denied entry, as glob and grep already do, so a listing of `$HOME` does not show `.ember` (`fr_x1_policy_denied_entry_is_not_listed_in_its_parent`). (2) A relative denied entry is an error (`PathPolicy::with_denied`, so `EMBER_NODE_DENY=rel` stops the node) instead of being dropped (fail open); the server's local node makes a relative `EMBER_DATA_DIR`, `EMBER_OSE_DATA_DIR` or `HOME` absolute against its working directory, as the server itself uses them, and logs a warning (`fr_x1_relative_denied_entries_are_refused_not_dropped`, `fr_x1_policy_denied_relative_entry_refuses_the_configuration`). (3) Every denied entry is resolved again at each check, so an entry created later, or a symlink planted at its place, is matched by its canonical form (`fr_x1_denied_entries_are_resolved_again_at_check_time`). A case variant (`.Ember`) on a case-insensitive volume is caught once the entry exists (canonicalising returns the stored name); a missing entry is matched by its bytes. (4) `secure_token_dir` uses one `O_NOFOLLOW | O_DIRECTORY` handle (row above). (5) The rows of #455 and #521 say CI. Written, not yet compiled or run. | #534 |
| FR-W4 (local node follow-ups, #474) | *[provisional, 2026-11-13]* (1) The local node client is `NodeClient::new_local`: its reqwest client is built with `no_proxy()`, kept by `with_deadline`, so `HTTP_PROXY`/`HTTPS_PROXY`/`ALL_PROXY` in the server environment never receive the Bearer token (`fr_w4_local_node_client_does_not_use_a_proxy_from_the_environment`, own test binary). (2) The token is 32 bytes from `getrandom` (`new_token`, `fr_w4_local_node_token_is_32_random_bytes`). (3) Dropping `Computers` aborts the accept task and closes the listener; connections already accepted are their own tasks and keep running until they end, so an attach in flight keeps working (`fr_w4_local_node_attach_in_flight_outlives_dropped_computers`). Accepted as is: the attach was authorised when it started, and it ends with the server process. (4) Lifetime: since FR-P6 (#26) the local node gets a state dir (`<data dir>/local-node`) and PTY keepers, so its terminals survive a server restart and are re-adopted (`LocalNodeOptions`). With `EMBER_NODE_KEEP_PTY=0` (and in tests without options) it has no state dir, starts no keeper and writes no record; its terminals are then children of the server and end with it (the PTY master closes, the shell gets SIGHUP). (5) At most `MAX_LOCAL_TERMS` (64) terminals run at once (`Terms::set_max_running`); one more create answers 409 `conflict`, a keyed create of a running session still answers it (`fr_p1_terms_running_session_cap_refuses_one_more` in `ember/node/tests/terms.rs`). A real node has no cap. (6) The WebSocket upgrade needs the Bearer token whatever the `Origin`; the node does not check `Origin`, because a browser cannot add `Authorization` to a WebSocket upgrade, so a foreign page cannot authenticate (`fr_w4_local_node_websocket_upgrade_needs_the_token_whatever_the_origin`); an attach aborted at several points of the handshake leaves the session running and attachable (`fr_w4_local_node_attach_aborted_in_flight_leaves_the_session_usable`). Tests in `ember/server/tests/fr_w4_local_node_hardening.rs` and `fr_w4_local_node_no_proxy.rs`, written, not yet compiled or run. | #474 |

## §L: Launcher and conversation UI (compose-rust; the IDE screens on dioxus-compose; no webview: E1, D9)

*[user, `INTENT.md` D9, 2026-10-26]* Every screen in this section is built on `compose-rust` directly (`ember/app/src/ui/cr/`) and launched by default (#220; `EMBER_CR_SHELL=light|dark` overrides the initial theme preference), except the Ember 에디터 screen (`FR-L31`, `ember/app/src/ui/editor.rs`, opened in its own `--editor <project> <folder>` process) and the workbench chrome (`FR-L14`, `ember/app/src/ui/workbench.rs`), which stay on `dioxus-compose`.

| ID | Requirement | Acceptance criteria |
| -- | ----------- | ------------------- |
| **FR-L1** | The main screen lists projects as selectable entries. *[user, `INTENT.md` D25, D27, 2026-10-25]* Each project is a two-level box: a header (status donut, name, counts, buttons for version control, IDE and settings, and "세션 열기") over an inset of member tiles named as team members ("ember-리더", "ember-구현"); clicking the header opens project settings (`FR-L26`), and "세션 열기" or a member tile opens the 세션 tab (`FR-L18`). Layout: `docs/DESIGN.md` §6.2. The "외 N명" deck is a card with overlapped avatars of the first four hidden members and the first three names (tests `fr_l1_deck_*`; CI compiles and runs them). | Renders from cached main-server data on cold start, before any network round trip completes; updates in place when fresh data arrives. |
| **FR-L2** | Each project shows its conversation sessions with a status: running, waiting for approval, finished-unread, finished, failed. | Status changes are pushed from the main server (`PR-1`), not polled per session. "Waiting for approval" outranks "running" when both apply. A finished session stays marked unread until opened. |

| **FR-G1** | *[provisional, #249, part of FR-L19]* The node reads a repository: `POST /git/status` returns branch, upstream, ahead, behind, last fetch age, local branches, and changed files (path, old path, index and worktree status). `POST /git/diff` returns the unified diff of one file or all, staged or not. A path that is not a repository answers 404 `not_a_repo`; a bare repository or dubious ownership answers 409 with its own message. `include_untracked` adds the untracked files to one diff call; output past 8 MiB answers 413. Messages are Korean with git's text as detail. | Tests run the HTTP API against a temp repository: a clean repo, a modified, an added, an untracked and a renamed file, and a non-repo directory. |
| **FR-G2** | *[provisional, #249]* The node stages and unstages through `POST /git/stage`, `/git/unstage` (whole files) and `/git/apply` (a unified patch to the index, forward or reverse, so a hunk or a line range is staged or unstaged). `POST /git/commit` commits the index with a summary and description, optionally amending. | A hunk staged by patch leaves the other hunks of the file unstaged; the commit holds only the staged lines; an empty index answers 409. |
| **FR-G3** | *[provisional, #249]* The node switches and creates branches (`/git/checkout`, `/git/branch`), and runs `/git/fetch`, `/git/pull` (fast-forward only) and `/git/push`; a failure carries a Korean message and git's text. A remote must be one of `git remote`; a network operation stops after 60 s (504 `timeout`) and never waits for a prompt; push and pull refuse a detached HEAD, pull needs an upstream and merges only the upstream's remote (another remote answers 409 before any fetch). A timeout or the output cap kills the whole process group of the network command. The user's own askpass and credential helper are kept; `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0` makes an unanswered prompt fail. `POST /git/conflicts` lists unmerged paths. | Tests use a local bare remote: push, fetch, ahead/behind change, a diverged pull answers 409 with the message. |
| **FR-G4** | *[provisional, #249]* The server exposes the same operations per project as `/api/projects/{name}/git/{op}`, forwarded to the project's computer node (the ops are those of FR-G1 to FR-G12, `prs`, `branches`, `pr`, `pr/review`, `pr/merge` and `pr/create` included, and `commit/show`, `revert` and `cherry-pick`, and `blame` of `FR-L31.8`); the client has typed calls, and 버전 관리 reads its snapshot and runs its actions through them. With `EMBER_DEMO_DATA=1` and `EMBER_SERVER_URL` unset, the demo source answers realistic git data. | Server route tests with a node stub; client tests against the server; the screen shows data instead of 준비 중입니다. |
| **FR-G5** | *[provisional, #260]* The node lists history: `POST /git/log` {repo, limit, skip, branch} returns commits newest first with sha, parents, author, time, subject and the refs pointing at each (branch, tag, HEAD). The 히스토리 section shows them as a list first; the lane graph waits for compose-rust#308. | Tests on a temp repo with a merge: parents of the merge commit, refs on tips, limit and skip, unborn branch answers an empty list. |
| **FR-G6** | *[provisional, #261]* The node keeps stashes: `/git/stash/list`, `/git/stash/push` (message, include untracked), `/git/stash/apply`, `/git/stash/pop`, `/git/stash/drop` by index. The 스태시 section lists and acts on them. | Tests: push then list shows the entry and a clean tree; pop restores; a conflicting pop answers 409 with a Korean message and keeps the entry. |
| **FR-G7** | *[provisional, #262]* The node resolves conflicts: `/git/conflict` {repo, path} returns the file as blocks (common text, ours, theirs, with labels); `/git/resolve` {repo, path, content} writes the resolution and stages it. The 충돌 section resolves block by block and then commits. | Tests: a merge conflict yields the blocks; resolving and committing clears `/git/conflicts`; a path that is not unmerged answers 409. |
| **FR-G8** | *[provisional, #328, part of FR-L19]* The node lists the repository's pull requests: `POST /git/prs` {repo, state: open\|closed\|merged\|all (default open), limit (default 30, 1 to 200)} runs the machine's own `gh pr list --json number,title,state,isDraft,author,headRefName,baseRefName,updatedAt,reviewDecision,statusCheckRollup,mergeable,url --limit N` in the repository. The credentials are gh's own on that computer; Ember stores no secret. `gh` runs with no prompt, no colour and no update notice (`GH_PROMPT_DISABLED=1`, `NO_COLOR=1`, `GH_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER=1`), under the 60 s network timeout (504) and the stdout cap of FR-G3. The answer lists per pull request: number, title, state (open, closed, merged), draft, author login, head and base branch, updated time, review decision (approved, changes_requested, review_required, none), a checks summary (passed, failed, pending counts from `statusCheckRollup`: SUCCESS, NEUTRAL and SKIPPED pass; FAILURE, ERROR, CANCELLED, TIMED_OUT and ACTION_REQUIRED fail; anything else is pending) and a merge gate (`can_merge` and a Korean reason). Per FR-L19 a pull request can be merged only when it is open, not a draft, free of conflicts, every check passed and it is approved. A missing `gh`, a `gh` that is not logged in and a repository with no GitHub remote answer 409 with a Korean message and gh's text as detail, never 500 and never a hang; a path that is not a repository answers 404 `not_a_repo`. The server relays the op (FR-G4), the client has `git_prs`, and the 풀 리퀘스트 section lists them with state, draft, review and checks pills and the merge gate line. `EMBER_GH_BIN` names another program than `gh` (tests). | Node tests (`ember/node/tests/git_prs.rs`, `git_prs_missing.rs`) with a fake `gh`: list parsing and arguments, merge gate (approved and green merges; a failed check names CI; no approval names 승인), check classes, gh errors 409, gh missing 409, non-repo 404; server relay test; client test; app demo and view-model tests. |
| **FR-G9** | *[provisional, part of FR-L19]* The node lists local branches: `POST /git/branches` {repo} runs one `git for-each-ref refs/heads` and answers per branch: name, `is_current`, `is_default` (the branch `refs/remotes/<remote>/HEAD` points to, trying `origin` first; else `main` or `master` if present; else none), `upstream` (short name or null), `ahead` and `behind` against the upstream (0 and 0 with `upstream: null` when none, and 0 and 0 when the upstream is gone), the last commit's short `last_sha`, `last_subject` and `last_time` (ISO 8601 committer time), and `worktree` (absolute path of the worktree that has the branch checked out, null if none; the main checkout counts). The list is sorted current first, then default, then by last commit time descending. Errors are those of the other git ops: a path that is not a repository answers 404 `not_a_repo` with a Korean message. The server relays the op (FR-G4), the client has `git_branches`, and the 브랜치 and 팀 작업 tabs of 버전 관리 read it. | Unit tests in `ember/node/src/git_branches.rs` (`fr_g9_*`: track parser incl. gone, line splitter with `|` in subjects, default fallback, ordering); node tests (`ember/node/tests/git_branches.rs`) on temp repos with a local bare remote: ahead 2 behind 1, tracked and untracked branches, linked worktree path, default, gone upstream, current first, non-repo 404; server relay test; client decode test; app demo test. |
| **FR-G10** | *[provisional, #483]* The node reads one pull request: `POST /git/pr` {repo, number} runs `gh pr view N --json number,title,body,state,isDraft,author,headRefName,headRefOid,baseRefName,url,reviewDecision,mergeable,reviews,comments,statusCheckRollup,reviewRequests,additions,deletions,changedFiles,updatedAt` under the rules of FR-G8 (gh's own credentials, no prompt, the 60 s timeout, the output cap, Korean 409 for a missing or logged-out `gh` or a repository with no GitHub remote, 404 `not_a_repo`). The number must be 1 to 999999 (else 400). The answer holds the list fields of FR-G8 (with the same merge gate; `checks` stays the passed, failed and pending count) and: `body` (cut at 20000 characters), `check_runs` [{name, state: passed\|failed\|pending, detail_url?}] (same classes as FR-G8), `reviews` [{author, state: approved\|changes_requested\|commented\|dismissed\|pending, body cut at 2000 characters, submitted_at}], `comments` [{author, body cut at 2000 characters, created_at}] (oldest first, newest last, at most the newest 50), `requested_reviewers` (logins; a team's slug), `additions`, `deletions`, `changed_files` and `head_sha` (the head commit, 40 hex characters). A cut text ends in `...`. | Node tests (`ember/node/tests/git_pr_ops.rs`, `fr_g10_*`, `git_prs_missing.rs`) with a fake `gh`: mapping with all check and review states, a 5000-character comment cut, 60 comments cut to 50 newest last, bad number 400, gh error 409, gh missing 409; server relay; client decode; demo tests. |
| **FR-G11** | *[provisional, #483]* The node reviews and merges through `gh`. `POST /git/pr/review` {repo, number, action: approve\|request_changes\|comment, body?, confirm: true} runs `gh pr review N --approve\|--request-changes\|--comment [--body=...]`; `request_changes` and `comment` need a non-empty body (400 in Korean). `POST /git/pr/merge` {repo, number, method: merge\|squash\|rebase, delete_branch, head_sha, confirm: true} answers 400 "확인한 헤드가 필요합니다." when `head_sha` is missing or empty (the caller must send the head SHA it displayed), before any `gh` call; it then reads the pull request again and answers 409 with the Korean merge-gate reason of FR-G8 when `can_merge` is false, without running `gh pr merge`. When `head_sha` (the head the user saw; 40 hex characters, else 400) differs from the head just read, it answers 409 "풀 리퀘스트가 바뀌었습니다. 다시 불러온 뒤 병합하세요." and merges nothing. Otherwise it runs `gh pr merge N --merge\|--squash\|--rebase --match-head-commit=<sha> [--delete-branch]` (the given sha), so a push after the read is never merged unreviewed. `--delete-branch` is gh's: it deletes the remote branch, and when the merged branch is checked out in the project's working directory gh may also delete the local branch and switch the checkout to the base branch; the screen says "원격 브랜치를 삭제합니다" only when the user chose it and answers `{number, merged: true, method}`. It never passes `--admin` or `--auto`, so the gate cannot be bypassed. Both need `confirm: true`, else 400 "확인이 필요합니다." and nothing reaches `gh`, so a stray request cannot act. Texts the caller gives are one argv entry each, in the `--flag=value` form, so a value that starts with `-` is never read as a flag; there is no shell. | `fr_g11_*` node tests with a fake `gh` that records its argv: `--approve` passed; `--request-changes` and `--comment` with the body as a `--body=` value; no body 400; no `confirm` 400 and gh not called; merge refused with the CI reason when a check failed and the recorded calls hold no `pr merge`; merge passes `--squash --match-head-commit=<sha> --delete-branch` when allowed and never `--admin` or `--auto`; a stale `head_sha` 409 without `pr merge`; bad `head_sha` 400; missing or empty `head_sha` 400 and gh not called (`fr_g11_merge_without_a_head_sha_is_400_and_never_calls_gh`); server relay of `pr/merge`; client calls; demo tests. Screen: the merge request carries the head SHA of the detail the user saw (`fr_g11_merge_body_carries_the_head_sha`); a detail without a head SHA disables 병합 with "헤드를 확인할 수 없어 병합할 수 없습니다" (`fr_g11_merge_is_disabled_without_a_head_sha`); a 409 "풀 리퀘스트가 바뀌었습니다..." shows that text and reloads the detail. At the wide layout the detail scrolls and the review box and 병합 stay pinned below it (`fr_l19_pr_wide_layout_pins_the_review_box_and_merge_below_the_scroll`); the compact layout ends with room for the floating tab bar. The selected pull request in the list has a border. |
| **FR-G12** | *[provisional, #483]* `POST /git/pr/create` {repo, title, body?, base?, head?, draft, confirm: true} runs `gh pr create --title=T --body=B [--base=] [--head=] [--draft]` (the body is always passed, so `gh` never opens an editor). The title is required, one line, at most 256 characters (else 400); the body is optional (at most 60000 characters; control characters other than newline, carriage return and tab are 400, as in a review body). `base` and `head` follow `git check-ref-format` (no `..`, `@{`, leading `-`, trailing `/`, `.` or `.lock`, whitespace, control characters or `~ ^ : ? * [ \`; else 400), except that `head` may be one `owner:branch` (a fork; owner of letters, digits, `-`, `_`, `.`). When `head` is given, the node first runs `gh pr list --head=<head> --state open --json number` and answers 409 "이미 열린 풀 리퀘스트가 있습니다: #N" if one exists; without `head` gh itself refuses a duplicate. The node pushes nothing: `head` must already exist on the remote, else gh's own error is shown in Korean (409 with gh's text as detail). The answer is `{number, url}`, parsed from the pull request url `gh` prints; no url in the output answers 500. `confirm: true` is required as in FR-G11. | `fr_g12_*` node tests with a fake `gh`: title, body, base, head and draft passed; the minimal call; empty or over-long title 400; no `confirm` 400; bad refs 400 and `owner:branch` allowed for head only; a body with a control character 400; an open pull request for the head 409; a body or title starting with `-` passed as a value; gh error 409; no url 500; client call; demo test. |
| **FR-G14** | *[provisional, #484]* The node reads one commit for the 히스토리 detail panel: `POST /git/commit/show` {repo, sha}. `sha` must be 7 to 40 hex characters (else 400); the node resolves it with `git rev-parse --verify --quiet <sha>^{commit}` and answers 404 `not_found` in Korean for an unknown one (404 `not_a_repo` outside a repository). The answer holds `sha` (full), `short` (7), `subject`, `body` (cut at 20000 characters), `author` (no email), `time` (author time, ISO 8601 strict), `parents` (full shas), `refs` (the same names and kinds as FR-G5) and `files` [{path, old_path?, status: added\|modified\|deleted\|renamed\|copied\|type_changed, additions, deletions, binary}], `files_total` and `truncated` (at most 500 files listed; a commit whose file lists exceed the 8 MiB output cap answers 413 "변경된 파일이 너무 많아 목록을 만들 수 없습니다."). Control characters (C0, DEL, C1, ESC) are stripped from `subject`, `body` (newline and tab stay) and `author`. The files come from `git diff-tree --name-status -r -M -z` and `--numstat` (binary files count 0/0 with `binary: true`); a merge commit is compared with its first parent, the root commit with the empty tree. Starting a branch from a commit is the existing `/git/branch` with `from` set to the sha (no new op). | Pure parser tests and control-character stripping (`fr_g14_*` in `ember/node/src/git_commit.rs`: name-status incl. renames and copies, numstat with binary and renames, join, sha check, body cut); node tests (`ember/node/tests/git_commit.rs`, `fr_g14_*`) on temp repositories: ordinary commit with add, modify, delete and rename, binary file, merge commit, root commit, unknown sha 404, bad sha 400, non-repository 404, branch from a sha; 600 files give `truncated` with 500 listed; control characters stripped; server relay; client decode; demo tests (`fr_g14_demo_*`). |
| **FR-G15** | *[provisional, #484]* The node reverts and cherry-picks a commit: `POST /git/revert` and `POST /git/cherry-pick` {repo, sha, confirm: true}. A bad sha (as FR-G14) is 400, and `confirm` missing or false is 400 "확인이 필요합니다." before the repository is opened. An unknown commit is 404 `not_found`. The tracked work tree must be clean (`git status --porcelain --untracked-files=no` empty), else 409 "작업 중인 변경이 있어 되돌릴 수 없습니다. 먼저 커밋하거나 스태시하세요." and nothing changes. Revert runs `git revert --no-edit <sha>` (`-m 1` for a merge commit); cherry-pick runs `git cherry-pick <sha>` and refuses a merge commit with 400 "병합 커밋은 지원하지 않습니다.". Git runs with `GIT_EDITOR=true` and no prompts. Both run with `-c core.hooksPath=/dev/null`, so the user's hooks never run through the API. Before it starts, the node refuses with 409 "진행 중인 git 작업이 있습니다. 먼저 끝내거나 중단하세요." when `CHERRY_PICK_HEAD`, `REVERT_HEAD`, `MERGE_HEAD`, `sequencer`, `rebase-merge` or `rebase-apply` exists under the git directory (found with `git rev-parse --git-path`, so worktrees work), and leaves that operation untouched. When git fails after the operation started, the node runs `git revert --abort` or `git cherry-pick --abort` and checks its exit code: on success HEAD, the index and the tree are as before; if the abort fails, the answer is 409 "되돌리기를 취소하지 못했습니다. 저장소에 진행 중인 작업이 남아 있을 수 있으니 터미널에서 git status를 확인하세요.". A failure before the operation started (a held `index.lock`) runs no abort. Every 409 text is Korean and carries no git text (git's text goes only to a debug log): a conflict is "충돌이 발생해 취소했습니다.", an empty result "적용할 변경이 없어 건너뛰었습니다.", missing identity "git 사용자 이름과 이메일이 설정되어 있지 않습니다.", another git process "다른 git 작업이 진행 중입니다. 잠시 뒤 다시 시도하세요.", anything else "git 작업에 실패했습니다.". Success answers `{new_sha}` (the new HEAD). | Node tests (`fr_g15_*`): revert of the last commit makes a revert commit; revert of a merge; cherry-pick from another branch applies; a dirty tree is 409 and changes nothing; a conflicting cherry-pick and revert are aborted (tree clean, HEAD unchanged); no English git text in the 409 bodies; a failed abort is never reported as cancelled (`abort_outcome` unit test, held `index.lock` test); a paused cherry-pick and marker files are refused and left in place; missing identity; a post-commit hook is not run; missing `confirm` 400 with no repository lookup; cherry-pick of a merge 400; unknown sha 404; server relay; client calls; demo tests (`fr_g15_demo_*`). |
| **FR-G16** | *[provisional, #382]* The node reports the merge state for the conflict screen banner: `POST /git/merge` {repo} answers `{in_progress, from, into, conflict_files, can_commit}`. `in_progress` is true when `MERGE_HEAD` exists under the git directory (found with `git rev-parse --git-path`, so worktrees work). `from` is the branch or ref being merged in: the first quoted name of the first line of `MERGE_MSG` (`Merge branch 'feat/x' into main` gives `feat/x`), else `git name-rev --name-only` of `MERGE_HEAD`, else null (best effort). `into` is the current branch (null on a detached HEAD). Both names are cleaned of control characters and cut at 100 characters. `conflict_files` is the number of unmerged paths (the `FR-G3` conflicts logic). `can_commit` is `in_progress` and `conflict_files == 0`. Outside a repository the answer is 404 `not_a_repo`. Read only, no lock. | Unit tests (`fr_g16_*` in `ember/node/src/git_merge.rs`: name from `MERGE_MSG` lines, control characters and the 100 character cut); node tests (`ember/node/tests/git_merge.rs`, `fr_g16_*`) on temp repositories: no merge, a conflicting merge before and after resolving, the `MERGE_HEAD` fallback for `from`, a non-repository 404; server relay; client decode; demo tests (`fr_g16_demo_*`). |
| **FR-G17** | *[provisional, #382]* The node cancels or finishes a merge in progress: `POST /git/merge/abort` and `POST /git/merge/commit` {repo, confirm: true}. `confirm` missing or false is 400 "확인이 필요합니다." before the repository is opened. Without `MERGE_HEAD` both answer 409 "진행 중인 병합이 없습니다.". Abort runs `git -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null merge --abort`, checks the exit code (a failure is 409 "병합을 취소하지 못했습니다. 저장소에 진행 중인 작업이 남아 있을 수 있으니 터미널에서 git status를 확인하세요.") and answers `{ok: true}`; the tree and index are as before the merge. Commit answers 409 "충돌이 남아 있어 병합 커밋을 만들 수 없습니다." while unmerged paths remain (nothing changes), else runs `git -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null commit --no-edit` (`GIT_EDITOR=true`) and answers `{new_sha}`. A failed commit maps through the `FR-G15` failure table (a missing identity is "git 사용자 이름과 이메일이 설정되어 있지 않습니다.") and leaves the merge in progress. No answer carries git text (it goes only to a debug log). Both take the write lock; the user's hooks never run through the API. Picking all blocks at once (모두 현재 것, 모두 들어오는 것) is a client-side action on the blocks of `FR-G7` and needs no API. | Node tests (`fr_g17_*` in `ember/node/tests/git_merge.rs`, `git_merge_identity.rs`): abort restores the tree and clears `MERGE_HEAD`; commit after resolving makes a merge commit with two parents; commit with conflicts left is 409 and changes nothing; abort and commit without a merge are 409; missing `confirm` is 400 with no repository lookup; no English git text; hooks are not run (commit and abort); missing identity; server relay; client calls; demo tests (`fr_g17_demo_*`). |
| **FR-G13** | *[provisional, `INTENT.md` D41, #498]* The node summarises one repository for the GitHub card: `POST /git/github` {repo} runs the machine's own `gh` (argv list, no shell, timeout and output cap as `FR-G8`) and answers the repository name (`owner/name`), the default branch, the signed-in account, the number of open pull requests, the number of open pull requests whose review the account is asked for, and the conclusions of the last 14 workflow runs on the default branch (newest first: success, failure, cancelled, pending). The server aggregates the user's projects: `GET /api/integrations/github` lists each distinct repository once (the repository of every project's newest unarchived session cwd, found by the node), calls the node per repository, and answers `state` (`on`, `warn` for signed out or insufficient scope, `off` for no `gh`), the account, per-repository summaries, totals (repositories, open pull requests, reviews waiting, CI pass percent; each sums the known values and is null when nothing is known, never an invented 0) and per-repository Korean errors. One refresh in flight, repositories asked 4 at a time under a 20 second deadline (a repository that has not answered is listed with 시간이 초과되었습니다), answers cached 90 seconds (10 seconds when every call failed). The server cleans names again. | **FR-L19** | *[user, `INTENT.md` D34, 2026-10-25]* **버전 관리** shows a project's repository at GitHub Desktop level: repository, branch and remote bar (pull/push/fetch, ahead/behind); changes with line and hunk staging and commit (amend); history graph; branches; pull requests with checks, reviews and line comments; stash; conflict resolution; team work per member (branch, worktree, computer). FR-L19 branch and stash follow-ups (#558) [user]: The branch list filters names without case sensitivity. Each default branch row has a 기본 pill, and ahead and behind bars have descriptive text. Branch switching ignores repeated taps while a request is pending and for 600 ms after it starts. The branch pane warns that switching, including create and switch, can carry uncommitted changes. The stash-now control says that untracked files are excluded. The disabled create-and-switch control remains legible in the light theme. The branch popover of the repository bar opens with a 브랜치 찾기 field that filters the list the same way, and says 검색 결과가 없습니다 when nothing matches (#513; test `fr_l19_branch_popover_search_filters_ignoring_case`, written, not compiled by the author). Tests `fr_l19_*` cover these decisions and the rendered branch pane; written, not yet compiled or run.

FR-L19 team work cards (#568) [provisional]: Each worktree card in **팀 작업** shows the assigned member's character (or an unassigned avatar), name, extra shared-worktree member count (`+N`), leader pill, session title and harness subtitle, state pill, an inset box with branch (and 현재 pill), worktree folder (`워크트리`), computer (`컴퓨터`, showing `알 수 없음` when the session record does not carry a computer, or a demo computer `mac-mini` in sample/demo mode), and dirty status (`깨끗함` or `변경 N개` for the current checkout when snapshot status is loaded, and `확인 안 됨` for other worktrees whose status is not read), the last commit line, and action controls: the PR chip (`PR 확인 중` while loading, `PR 확인 실패` on fetch error, `PR 없음` when none matches, or `#N 통과` / `#N 실패` / `#N 진행 중` / `#N 병합됨` / `#N 닫힘` matched across all pull request states; clicking `#N` selects that pull request and opens **풀 리퀘스트**), `충돌 해결` (when the current checkout has conflicts), `브랜치 보기`, and `세션`. Rows sort with the leader's worktree first, then the main checkout, then other worktrees by branch name, and worktree matching normalizes macOS `/private/var`, `/private/tmp` and `/private/etc` prefixes case-insensitively. Tests `fr_l19_team_*` in `ember/app/src/ui/vcs.rs` and `ember/app/src/ui/cr/vcs.rs`.

| **FR-L20** | *[user, `INTENT.md` D26, 2026-10-25: "에이전트 메뉴를 하나 더 추가해서 거기에 하네스와 모델, 그리고 라우팅 등의 우리가 지원하는 기능들을 설정할 수 있도록 세팅해주자. 홈에 있던 사용량 표시도 같이 넣어주고."]* **에이전트** has sections: harnesses (installed, version, connection, run mode, Antigravity sandbox, resident shim `FR-S8`, per-session memory limit, default profile), models (supported models and efforts, defaults, plan availability, default permission mode), profiles (`FR-L17`), network (egress `FR-X6`), usage (`FR-U7` tokens, cost by harness and model, account rings). Draft. | Each setting is stored on the main server and takes effect for the next session. Re-detect finds a newly installed harness (`FR-A6`). Usage figures equal `FR-U7` totals; a harness without token reports shows "unknown". Unverified features carry their issue (egress #130). **Memory limit on the server (#311, FR-L20, FR-S8) [provisional, 2026-11-06, leader approval relayed by the supervisor; owner confirmation pending], written test first, not yet compiled or run:** the per-harness memory limit is stored on the main server, in MiB, in the generic table `harness_settings(harness, key, value, updated_ms)` (migration 28; this issue stores only the key `mem_limit_mb`; run mode, default profile and network follow the same rule later). `PUT /api/agents/{agent}/settings` takes exactly `{"mem_limit_mb": <integer> | null}`; `null` or `0` clears, otherwise the value must be 256 to 262144 MiB, else 400 `mem_limit_mb must be 0 (clear) or between 256 and 262144 MiB`; an unknown field, a missing `mem_limit_mb` or a body that is not an object is 422; a value that is not a number is 400; `{agent}` is a wire name as `AgentKind::parse` knows it (`claude-code`, `codex`, `antigravity`, a configured ACP name), an unknown one or `scripted` is 404. The answer is 200 `{agent, mem_limit_mb: <u64>|null, applies: "next_session"}`. `GET /api/agents/settings` answers `{settings: {<agent>: {mem_limit_mb}}}` for harnesses with a stored value only. Both routes sit in the token-protected router beside `/api/agents` and are never token-exempt. **The limit applies from the next fresh agent start**: a running session, and a session whose shim survives a server restart, keeps the limit its shim started with (the shim takes it as an argument at spawn and a live change is out of scope); a new session, a native resume after the shim died and a restart of the agent use the new value. The app says so beside the field. Clearing falls back to `EMBER_SESSION_MEM_LIMIT_MB`, then to no limit. At startup `Sessions::load_settings` (called from `main.rs` after the store and the sessions exist) loads every stored value; a value that is not a `u64`, is outside the range, or belongs to a harness name that no longer maps to a known agent (an ACP agent removed from the configuration) is ignored with a `warn` that names the harness only. `Sessions::set_mem_limit_persisted` validates the range, stores, then sets the memory value; `set_mem_limit_mb` stays memory-only. **The lower bound 256 is an UNMEASURED assumption** (nobody measured the smallest memory a real agent needs; a measurement of Claude Code, Codex and Antigravity is a follow-up); the upper bound 262144 (256 GiB) only stops typos and byte overflow. Tests (`ember/server/tests/fr_l20_agent_settings.rs`, `store.rs`): `fr_l20_harness_setting_roundtrip`, `fr_l20_harness_setting_survives_reopen`, `fr_l20_harness_settings_with_key_lists_only_that_key`, `fr_l20_load_settings_applies_a_stored_value`, `fr_l20_load_settings_ignores_bad_rows`, `fr_l20_set_mem_limit_persisted_writes_store_and_memory`, `fr_l20_set_mem_limit_persisted_refuses_out_of_range`, `fr_l20_put_then_get`, `fr_l20_null_and_zero_clear`, `fr_l20_range_edges`, `fr_l20_unknown_field_and_missing_key_are_422`, `fr_l20_wrong_type_is_400`, `fr_l20_unknown_agent_and_scripted_are_404`, `fr_s8_settings_routes_need_the_token`, `fr_s8_settings_paths_are_not_token_exempt` (`auth.rs`), `fr_s8_new_session_gets_the_stored_limit_and_a_live_one_keeps_its_own`, `fr_s8_limit_survives_a_rebuilt_sessions`. **New-member defaults on the server (#481, #670, FR-L20) [provisional]:** the new-member defaults (`새 팀원 기본값`: default harness, model, effort and permission mode) are stored in `harness_settings` under `harness = "_defaults"` and keys `agent`, `model`, `effort` and `mode`. `GET /api/agents/defaults` answers 200 `{agent, model, effort, mode}` (each a string or `null`). `PUT /api/agents/defaults` takes a JSON object with optional `agent`, `model`, `effort` and `mode` (unknown field or non-object body is 422; a non-string non-null value is 400; `null` or `""` clears that field, omitted fields keep their stored value, and clearing `agent` without giving `model` also clears `model`); `agent` must be a known non-`scripted` wire name as `AgentKind::parse` knows it (`claude-code`, `codex`, `antigravity` or a configured ACP name, else 400), `model` 1 to 128 characters without control characters (else 400), `effort` one of `Low`, `Medium` or `High` (else 400), and `mode` one of `ask`, `edits`, `plan` or `bypass` (else 400). Both routes sit in the token-protected router beside `/api/agents/settings`. The app reads `GET /api/agents/defaults` and `GET /api/agents/settings` on refresh, writes `PUT /api/agents/defaults` when the owner picks a default harness, model, effort or mode on 에이전트 (`모델`), and writes `PUT /api/agents/{agent}/settings` when the owner adjusts the per-harness memory limit slider on `하네스`. Tests: `fr_l20_defaults_*` in `ember/server/tests/fr_l20_agent_settings.rs`, `ember/app/src/server.rs`, `ember/app/src/ui/agents.rs` and `ember/app/src/ui/cr/agents.rs`. |

An override whose account was deleted is not used and reads as `null`, the account delete does not touch projects. Validation reuses `validate_settings` (name rules, repository without whitespace, branch ref rules) and an empty branch field means `develop` (the hint 비워 두면 develop을 씁니다 shows under it; `default_branch` is sent only when a repository was given or a branch typed). A failed create maps 409 `name_taken` to "이미 있는 이름입니다", 403 `protected_project` to "기본 프로젝트와 같은 이름은 쓸 수 없습니다" and anything else to a fixed sentence, never raw text (tests `fr_l26_new_project_empty_branch_means_develop`, `fr_l26_new_project_create_error_maps_name_taken`); Enter in a field creates when the draft is valid, editing a field clears the failure label, and the repository value is never logged. If the PATCH fails the project is kept and the sheet closes with "프로젝트는 만들었지만 저장소와 캐릭터 설정은 저장하지 못했습니다. 프로젝트 설정에서 다시 저장해 주세요"; a failed create keeps the sheet open. 취소, X and the backdrop ask the unsaved-changes question once anything was typed (`SettingsGuard`). One icon serves every computer kind because node does not report the kind (FR-X7).
